# Architect answers — A-S5-overview (2026-10-14)

## Registration and selection

Registered three open, sized S4-02 follow-on nodes in the registry and its `queue_baseline_nodes`: `I-price-equal-start-succession` (S), `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work` (M), and `I-stored-demo-provenance` (M). Each needs a Planner-written bounded S4-02 follow-on packet; no packet is written here. The existing Assumptions-history follow-on remains separate; its read acceptance now explicitly requires foreign-Site refusal. Q1 preserves recorded cutoff history on an equal-start future price replacement. Q2 durably enqueues/retries consequence work on accepted evidence and is an AND prerequisite of `I-site-overview-read`; it must close before S5-01 consumes current money, but the architecture decision may proceed now. M reflects the transaction, selector/worker, idempotency and end-to-end late-power walkthrough, rather than a single small edit. Q3 stores server-authorized, tenant/Site-scoped authored source designation and traces accepted evidence into Finding/consequence reads. Registry `delivery_plan.waves.W1.exit_requires` and `real_pilot_requires`/`physical_peer_gate` name Q3 as a W1-exit and first-real-pilot gate. These metadata gates require coordinator enforcement at those checkpoints; the current checker validates node links but does not itself enforce either checkpoint.

Registry validation passed (222 nodes). A fresh `--next-work` report at `.agent/next-work-2026-10-14c.json` was generated and `--verify-report` passed before resolving the selected architecture node. It selected `A-S5-overview` 2.198 versus `A-action-lifecycle` 2.009, gap 8.60%; the published 5–10% tie-break chooses S5 with notice. This is not a <=5% owner contest. The Q2 node was eligible at 1.998. The report is a pre-decision snapshot and must be regenerated after this decision before selecting another task.

## S5-01 contract and Planner handoff

Decision `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md` is authoritative. Build one S5-01 read/UI slice for the selected half-open Site window: exact S3 assessment and S4 Finding/consequence IDs, coverage, accepted-source and analytic watermarks, model version, built time, freshness reason and exact evidence/configuration/assumption links. Recompute or invalidate on late evidence, changed analytic/configuration/model basis and consequence jobs. Prove consistent selection after reload and date change. Keep old revisions addressable in stale/pending/failed states. The Planner must specify wire fields, projection versus equivalent scoped read, fixtures for assessed/Unknown/late-power/failed/unpriced/priced cases, exact routes, desktop/narrow states and independent review. `NO_PENDING_DISPATCH` alone is not CURRENT.

Cards: selected dispatch objective/status and coverage; selected-window timeline and operating context from accepted readings; optional bounded fuel-cost card. Unassessed objectives remain Unknown. Do not present unsupported service, battery condition, renewable opportunity, fuel balance, charging causality, maintenance allowance or recommendation as real. A money amount requires a `CURRENT PRICED` same-FindingRevision, same-window, current-basis consequence, with model-conditioned exposure wording, zero lower bound, assumption/evidence links and no overlapping sum. Ordinary 0/11 qualifying Findings yield no amount and a limiting-state link; the single authored P01-power positive case carries a visible Demonstration-data label and lineage. Show distinct no-amount `UNPRICED`, quantity unavailable, pending, stale, superseded, mixed/unknown-source and absent states. The current date-based demonstration marker is bounded fixture evidence only until Q3 lands.

`mini:1#a`, `mini:2#a`, `mini:4#a` are the reviewable S5-01 targets. The mock's blended money composition remains a G-05 gap; `mini:3#a/b` waits for S4-04 Site FindingIndex plus S5-02 empty/positive list integration. A known Finding card may link its exact revision, but no Site-wide list is claimed. S5-01 stays one slice; split only if Planner finds a concrete independent review/owner-checkpoint need, preserving the Q2 gate on the money-consuming read. The packet must retain `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED`.

## Owner checkpoints while absent

Build and fresh-review USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED slices, prepare a reproducible walkthrough, and hold merge/acceptance until the owner personally walks and records the visual checkpoint. This rule applies to this window and future owner-absent windows. Architect delegation covers sequence and contract/copy rulings, not the owner's eyes. Planning, unrelated non-UI phases and eligible G packets may proceed under the existing single-active-task rule. A dependent next slice may be prepared on a clearly stacked branch, without marking the predecessor accepted or merged. Once the owner returns, perform the pending walkthrough, address feedback and only then merge/promote the slice.

## Owner-facing summary of delegated decisions

1. Three missing follow-ons are now tracked: future price replacement at its start, automatic recalculation after late accepted readings, and stored proof that data was authored for a demonstration. The recalculation work is required before Overview money; stored proof is required before W1 exit or a real-Site pilot.
2. The next architecture selection is S5 Overview (8.60% ahead of Action lifecycle). # S6-02 — Later-window verification

Status: outline; refine after preceding task review, before activation
USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true
Milestone: S6 Work / Action / Verification
Depends on: S6-01
Branch: task/S6-02-verification
Planner: Codex, 2026-10-07

## Visible outcome

Site verification shows target, service/reserve guardrails and Verified, Ineffective or Inconclusive outcome.

## Persisted object and read contract

Immutable VerificationPlanRevision/Outcome and later-window analytic comparison read API.

## Demo acceptance

Planner names `I-sim-verification-cases`: use a baseline Lab run under the old effective Foundation and a separate later run under the S6-01 persisted policy revision. Produce a later public window with a target outcome, a guardrail failure and an inconclusive-evidence variant. Commit reports through the S2 gateway/ingestion path before evaluation. No policy change partway through one run is required; keep the fixed plan revision, applied-change event and both runs' public observations separately traceable.

Evaluate later committed evidence against frozen baseline and plan; fail a guardrail; reopen both windows and later recurrence.

## Missing/conflicting evidence

Missing baseline, weak coverage or conflicting later evidence yields Inconclusive.

## Evidence lineage

Outcome resolves plan, baseline/later assessments and accepted SourceEnvelope IDs.

## Review and refinement

S6-02 owns `I-sim-verification-cases`, `D-verification-window`, `I-verification-plan-read/ui`, `I-verification-read/ui` and `I-action-evidence-read/ui`. These gate `verification:1-3`, effect `changes:1#b` (later-window effect of the applied change) and `changes:2`, `recommendation:2-3`, `action-plan:1` and `action-evidence:1`. The moved child views select the frozen `plan_revision_id`, predeclared target/guardrails and required evidence, accepted later-window IDs and explicit missing/inconclusive states. Compare exact Action child-view app/mock desktop and narrow behavior; no panel is delivered until its read and fixture paths are reviewed.

Reviewer opens the selected-window surface and read API, verifies storage/reload and the state above, and follows lineage. Apply shared gates in the current task queue. Before implementation, refine exact fixture, wire/API fields, migration seam and focused checks using the reviewed predecessor and accepted architecture/technical design. This outline is not permission to change those contracts.

## Defer

Global Actions index and causal claims stronger than the comparison supports.

Decision: `D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis`. The owner selected `A-S4-financial` in the 2026-10-13 next-work contest; the Codex Architect resolves its S4-02 contract in `.agent/architect-A-S4-financial-answers.md`. A Site-scoped `FinancialAssumptionRevision` has immutable scope/key/revision, ISO currency and canonical unit, Decimal value, explicit authored/contract/recorded/scenario basis, half-open valid time and recorded/superseded time. Writes derive human `author_id` and actor/authorization metadata from a verified server principal under `A-actor-identity`; absent authentication disables human editing, while an explicitly labelled server-authorized SYSTEM fixture may publish with service/job provenance. No default price, silent tenant fallback, overlapping current validity, client-asserted author or unlabelled retroactive edit is permitted. The first key is Site diesel price per litre; maintenance needs its own rate/source and matching quantity and cannot be inferred as actual expenditure from candidate runtime.

S4-02 may derive a modelled, assumption-conditioned **potential fuel-cost exposure** only for an exact supported mini-grid FindingRevision and its selected assessment/evidence/configuration basis. `dispatch-3.1` publishes no fuel-saving quantity: a separately reviewed versioned fuel derivation must use accepted generator-power coverage for candidate segments and the selected Foundation's L/kWh consumption property, with explicit uncertainty/bounds and no private Lab truth, unsupported interpolation or pricing of Indeterminate/Necessary time. Candidate time proves no saved liters. The lower bound on potentially avoidable fuel is zero; an upper amount is shown only under a defensible stated model bound. A price valid for the entire consequence window and known at the selection cutoff multiplies unrounded compatible Decimal quantity bounds; displayed lows round down and highs up to currency minor units. No price yields `UNPRICED` with null amount, never zero; unsupported quantity, mismatched unit/currency/period/basis, stale projection and historical supersession receive distinct honest states.

Each append-only `FinancialConsequenceRevision` stores exact FindingRevision, primary and supporting assessment revisions, technical quantity bounds and source/basis digest, every assumption revision used, formula version, overlap group, half-open window, amount bounds/currency and limitations. An explicit backdated price correction valid over the old window appends a new assumption and consequence for the same FindingRevision and quantity; a future price does not reprice yesterday, and neither edit mutates the Finding or pinned old consequence. S4-02 persists and reads Site-scoped assumption/consequence current and exact history with actor, validity, lineage, pagination and typed refusal/pending states. After review it may support `financials:1#a/2#a` and `assumptions:1#a/3#a`; `mini:4#a` waits for S5 composition. Trade-offs, maintenance without a source, `financial_scenario`, FX, extrapolation and estate totals remain open. `A-money-comparability` stays outside W1 ranking and is required before cross-option/Site aggregation. Planner splits the outline into two reviewable M slices (assumption publication/history and honest unpriced bridge; then modelled quantity/consequence/repricing/UI), with the S4-02 owner checkpoint after independent review.

For G-03â€“G-05, `missing_kind` names the first absent authority: `contract` when record type, author/validity semantics or read rule is undefined; `data_source` when that contract exists but the producer/records are missing; `decision`, `build` and `visual` retain their distinct reviewed-choice, implementation and presentation meanings. This clarifies the commissioning example in `D-2026-10-12-mock-gap-closure-mechanism`: `G-readiness-commissioning` is now `contract/absent` because no commissioning record type exists. Addendum to `D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis` (S4-02b `fuel-cost-1`): The first reviewed fuel model may use immutable, dimensionless `sfc_upper_multiplier = 1.10` as an expressly conditional assumption that actual L/kWh does not exceed 1.10 times the selected Foundation SFC. Its version records the value, inequality, scope, source, reviewer/date, formula and immutable lineage; it is not a measured or calibrated physical bound. For each exact FindingRevision, quantify only observed Candidate spans fully covered by accepted, good, nonoverlapping generator-power mean intervals, each wholly contained within contiguous Candidate time and carrying one unambiguous positive SFC throughout. Count each distinct interval once: `quantity_low = 0 L`, `quantity_high = 1.10 Ã— Î£(mean kW Ã— full interval hours Ã— SFC L/kWh)`. Never use dispatch interval `upper_minutes`, a segment `max mean Ã— duration`, or prorating of an interval that crosses non-Candidate time. Any failed coverage, containment, power or SFC condition makes the whole consequence `QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE` with no amount. The upper is a model-conditioned potential fuel-cost exposure, not measured fuel saved or a physical maximum. Independent model review and the planned owner UI checkpoint still apply. `FindingRevision` and `FinancialConsequenceRevision` refer to the exact assessment revision they explain. The evidence drawer traverses `finding_revision â†’ assessment_revision â†’ analytic_run_evidence â†’ accepted_evidence â†’ envelope_text`, plus the bound Foundation/SignalBinding and assumption revisions. Deleting a source row is prohibited while a dependent claim exists. Evidence behind a conclusion is not a rendered explanation string.

### Read projections

Start with three transactionally rebuildable tables, not one table per mock screen:

| Table | PK and fields; example | Update rule / indexes |
FinancialConsequence {
  consequence_revision_id, finding_revision_id, assessment_revision_id,
  assumption_revision_id?, consequence_type, quantity:{value,unit},
  amount:{low,high,currency}?, basis, formula_version, overlap_group,
  window, limitations
}
```

`QueryScope.window` is half-open `[start,end)` and required for analytic/product queries. `knowledge_at` makes historical audits reproducible; normal UI defaults to current server time. `selected_revision_id` freezes a drill-down even if the surrounding window changes. `AnalyticInputSet.input_sha256` hashes sorted evidence IDs **and their content digests**, resolved configuration/binding revisions, window, analytic/model version and canonical role coverage. The financial consequence has a separate basis digest including its assumption revisions. Missing required roles produce a bounded Unknown/Indeterminate assessment with explicit reason; the analytic never reads world state or a Lab run ID. `FinancialConsequence` has no amount when the technical quantity is supported but price/demand is not. `Measured`, `Modelled`, `Extrapolated` and `Scenario` are distinct bases; UI must expose them.

## 4. Transaction and processing contracts

1. **Register configuration.** Publish Foundation and SignalBinding versions together under Site lock. Validate site type, role vocabulary, units, asset ownership and non-overlapping current valid periods. Persist canonical snapshot hash for authenticated gateway download; no detached snapshot signature or key-rotation system is required in S1â€“S7. Backdated publication enqueues affected windows for recomputation; do not change accepted evidence bytes.
2. **Receive batch.** TLS and gateway credential establish tenant/site/gateway/source. Enforce size/count limits before parsing; resolve external batch ID and digest. Identical retry returns stored receipt; conflicting retry records/refuses without touching prior decisions. A batch with wrong registered mode or configuration reference is refused as a whole.
3. **Decide records.** In one Postgres transaction, lock batch row; for each unprocessed ordinal parse strict envelope, verify identity/sequence and referenced mapping/Foundation validity at `source_time`, normalize with the registered as-accepted conversion, and insert acceptance or rejection plus disposition. Unique message key arbitrates concurrent duplicate deliveries. Equal digest is `DUPLICATE`; changed digest is `CONFLICT` and points to first accepted evidence. Update watermark and create/coalesce recompute jobs in the same commit. Chunk a large batch into short committed transactions; receipt exposes `IN_PROGRESS` until all ordinals are durable. A client retries the same batch/bytes and resumes from the persisted watermark. Return a terminal receipt only after all decisions commit.
4. **Assess.** Worker leases dirty site/domain/window jobs, selects accepted evidence by source time plus interval overlap at a declared knowledge cutoff, resolves the exact public configuration, builds `AnalyticInputSet`, and runs the versioned pure analytic. In one commit insert `AnalyticRun`, immutable assessment/finding/consequence revisions when basis changed, update projections and mark job done. Retry of same input hash is a no-op. Late or out-of-order evidence recomputes the affected and neighboring continuity window; it never mutates old revisions. Model deployment schedules explicit re-evaluation of supported windows.
5. **Verify.** ActionEvent records work. After a later closed window exists, evaluate the frozen plan against accepted evidence, target and each guardrail. Missing coverage is `INCONCLUSIVE`; worsening service prevents `VERIFIED` even if cost falls. Link outcome to exact analytic run/evidence and surface any later superseding outcome.

**Initial API contract:** `POST /api/ingestion/batches` returns terminal `200` receipt or `202 IN_PROGRESS` receipt; `GET /api/ingestion/batches/{id}` returns the same durable state. `409` is reserved for same batch ID/different bytes or registered identity conflict, `422` for malformed contract, `401/403` for authentication/authorization. Normal reads use `GET /api/sites/{id}/evidence`, `/evidence/records/{id}`, `/overview`, `/performance/{view}`, `/findings/{id}` with `from`, `to` and optional `revision_id`; list reads paginate by stable `(source_time,id)` or `(created_at,id)` cursor. All responses include scope/window, revision IDs, evidence coverage and projection watermark. The UI treats a lagging projection as pending, not healthy. Lab Commit calls this same HTTP boundary with a registered simulated gateway; no privileged product-write endpoint exists.

**Gateway diagnostic API:** `POST /api/gateways/{gateway_id}/diagnostics` authenticates the registered gateway/site, checks `GatewayDiagnosticsV1` identity/hash and inserts `gateway_diagnostic` with server `received_at`; an identical retry returns the existing row. `409` refuses identity reuse with different bytes, `422` malformed fields, and `401/403` wrong credential/scope. `GET /api/sites/{id}/gateway-health` returns last server contact, declared cadence, source-contact observations, queue age/depth, disk pressure, clock offset and an explicit `CURRENT|STALE|UNKNOWN` status with diagnostic ID and receive time. It makes no claim that the device or asset itself is healthy. S2-01c publishes locally; S2-02 owns central storage and read API; S7 composes the Site Health claim.

+# Architect answer: 0.54% next-work contest (2026-10-14)
+
+## Decision under owner delegation
+
+Choose `A-action-lifecycle` now. The report `.agent/next-work-2026-10-14d.json` gave `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work` 2.020 and `A-action-lifecycle` 2.009, a 0.54% gap that normally requires the owner. The owner's 08:40-10:40 +0300 delegation makes this an Architect decision. The recommended consequence work is essential: it gates S5-01's current money read. It still needs a bounded Planner packet and implementation/review. The Action decision can be completed in this call and removes the shared architecture gate from roughly fifteen W1 S6 core portions. It does not claim those portions delivered or remove their implementation, verification and owner gates. Resolve the contest by taking this bounded decision, then use the regenerated verified report for the next item; do not reuse the pre-decision ranking.
+
+The architecture ruling is `D-2026-10-14-action-lifecycle-and-policy-application` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`; registry node `A-action-lifecycle` is resolved with that evidence. A Finding-linked Action has stable identity and exact origin revision. Server-authorized, append-only ActionEvents establish acceptance, application attempts and history. A reserve-SOC change counts as applied only when S1 Foundation publish durably creates its new effective revision and a change artifact ties old/new revisions to the Action and event. Pending or failed application is visible without claiming an effective change. Applied work is not verified benefit. S6-02's frozen plan, later accepted evidence and guardrail verdict remain separate; `A-S6-verification` remains open. The Site ActionIndex and list remain S6-03 and `A-action-index`.
+
+## Planner refinement to take from this decision
+
+Refine `tasks/S6-01-action-change.md` against `tasks/S6-02-verification.md`, `tasks/S6-03-site-work-list.md`, the Action root/Recommendation/Changes/Execution/History/Verification screens in `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html` and its manifest, and `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md` sections on Action/ActionEvent, Foundation publication, verification and root ownership. Name exact Action, event and change-artifact fields; accepted FindingRevision and assessment/evidence links; canonical Action child routes and selected event/cutoff behavior; state-transition and refusal table; idempotent concurrent command handling; verified-operator versus clearly labelled SYSTEM fixture authorization; Foundation expected-revision/publish transaction and failed/pending retry semantics. Specify persisted positive, pending, failed, stale-command, wrong-Site and reload fixtures, including 1440/390 app/mock review. Keep outcome pending in S6-01. S6-02 later freezes the plan and proves two fixed-policy runs, accepted later window, target, failed guardrail and inconclusive case. S6-03 alone supplies the watermarked Site Work list. These are Planner requirements, not a claim that the outline packets have been activated.
+
+The next Planner packet for `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work`, when selected by the refreshed report, should stay a separate M-size, mostly non-UI S4-02 follow-on. It must cover transactionally durable enqueue on potentially relevant accepted Site/window evidence, scoped worker selection and retry/idempotency, latest eligible FindingRevision/basis checks, irrelevant-input no-op, no duplicate same-basis consequence, and an end-to-end late accepted generator-power walkthrough from stale/pending through new consequence revision and S5-read eligibility. Independent review is required. It does not need a separate owner visual checkpoint if it adds no UI or owner-facing copy; S5-01 retains its own `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` checkpoint. This packet cannot be treated as closed by a stale read alone.
+

+# Architect answer: 0.54% next-work contest (2026-10-14)
+
+## Decision under owner delegation
+
+Choose `A-action-lifecycle` now. The report `.agent/next-work-2026-10-14d.json` gave `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work` 2.020 and `A-action-lifecycle` 2.009, a 0.54% gap that normally requires the owner. The owner's 08:40-10:40 +0300 delegation makes this an Architect decision. The recommended consequence work is essential: it gates S5-01's current money read. It still needs a bounded Planner packet and implementation/review. The Action decision can be completed in this call and removes the shared architecture gate from roughly fifteen W1 S6 core portions. It does not claim those portions delivered or remove their implementation, verification and owner gates. Resolve the contest by taking this bounded decision, then use the regenerated verified report for the next item; do not reuse the pre-decision ranking.
+
+The architecture ruling is `D-2026-10-14-action-lifecycle-and-policy-application` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`; registry node `A-action-lifecycle` is resolved with that evidence. A Finding-linked Action has stable identity and exact origin revision. Server-authorized, append-only ActionEvents establish acceptance, application attempts and history. A reserve-SOC change counts as applied only when S1 Foundation publish durably creates its new effective revision and a change artifact ties old/new revisions to the Action and event. Pending or failed application is visible without claiming an effective change. Applied work is not verified benefit. S6-02's frozen plan, later accepted evidence and guardrail verdict remain separate; `A-S6-verification` remains open. The Site ActionIndex and list remain S6-03 and `A-action-index`.
+
+## Planner refinement to take from this decision
+
+Refine `tasks/S6-01-action-change.md` against `tasks/S6-02-verification.md`, `tasks/S6-03-site-work-list.md`, the Action root/Recommendation/Changes/Execution/History/Verification screens in `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html` and its manifest, and `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md` sections on Action/ActionEvent, Foundation publication, verification and root ownership. Name exact Action, event and change-artifact fields; accepted FindingRevision and assessment/evidence links; canonical Action child routes and selected event/cutoff behavior; state-transition and refusal table; idempotent concurrent command handling; verified-operator versus clearly labelled SYSTEM fixture authorization; Foundation expected-revision/publish transaction and failed/pending retry semantics. Specify persisted positive, pending, failed, stale-command, wrong-Site and reload fixtures, including 1440/390 app/mock review. Keep outcome pending in S6-01. S6-02 later freezes the plan and proves two fixed-policy runs, accepted later window, target, failed guardrail and inconclusive case. S6-03 alone supplies the watermarked Site Work list. These are Planner requirements, not a claim that the outline packets have been activated.
+
+The next Planner packet for `I-evidence-acceptance-consequence-work`, when selected by the refreshed report, should stay a separate M-size, mostly non-UI S4-02 follow-on. It must cover transactionally durable enqueue on potentially relevant accepted Site/window evidence, scoped worker selection and retry/idempotency, latest eligible FindingRevision/basis checks, irrelevant-input no-op, no duplicate same-basis consequence, and an end-to-end late accepted generator-power walkthrough from stale/pending through new consequence revision and S5-read eligibility. Independent review is required. It does not need a separate owner visual checkpoint if it adds no UI or owner-facing copy; S5-01 retains its own `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` checkpoint. This packet cannot be treated as closed by a stale read alone.
+
+## Owner-facing summary
+
+I selected the Action lifecycle decision in the near tie because it is ready to settle now and clears a common dependency for the later Work screens. The automatic recalculation after accepted late evidence remains mandatory before Overview can show current money, and its implementation still needs a Planner packet. No UI walkthrough is being marked complete during your absence; reviewed UI slices wait for your visual checkpoint before merge.

+S6-01 creates one immutable, tenant/Site-scoped Action identity from a reviewed persisted `FindingRevision` under the canonical Finding route. It pins the originating revision, selected assessment and accepted-evidence/configuration basis; a later Finding revision does not silently rewrite that origin. A Finding may have several Actions, but each accepted recommendation creates or idempotently returns one Action for its server-defined command key. The recommendation is a bounded proposed reserve-SOC policy trial with explicit purpose, limitation and source Finding; it is not a measured saving or verified outcome. The Action detail root is `/sites/{site}/actions/{action_id}` with stable child routes and exact `event_id` selection, and Site/Finding back-links. S6-01 may navigate from a known Finding or Action ID before the separate S6-03 Site ActionIndex exists. Hidden unsupported tabs must not masquerade as empty delivered views.
+
+Action state is derived from an append-only, server-ordered `ActionEvent` ledger. Record stable event ID, Action and Site, event type, `occurred_at`, server `recorded_at`, idempotency key, actor kind/ID, source channel, authorization basis and triggering command/job or content revision. Idempotent identical retries return the event; conflicting reuse and cross-Site references fail. Historical reads select a recorded cutoff and then `(recorded_at,id)` order; a backdated `occurred_at` is displayed as such but never rewrites the audit order. `PROPOSED`/`ACCEPTED`, application `PENDING`/`APPLIED`/`FAILED`, and later verification status are separate dimensions. Acceptance does not assert application; an applied change or completed work does not assert benefit. A failed application preserves its attempt, error and retry relation without an effective-policy claim. No human author, approval or attestation is inferred from a gateway credential, client field, local OS user or SYSTEM fixture. Under the existing `A-actor-identity` rule, human writes need a verified operator principal; otherwise only a clearly labelled, server-authorized SYSTEM demonstration command with stable service/job provenance is allowed, or the write stays unavailable.
+
+The supported S6-01 change is a controlled reserve-SOC edit through S1 Foundation publish, producing a new immutable effective `FoundationRevision` and an immutable change artifact that links Action, accepted recommendation, authorized command, old/new revision IDs, exact field/value/unit, validity and recorded time, and the application event. Validate the expected old revision and Site scope under the publish lock; reject stale, unauthorized or invalid edits and record a failed attempt without claiming the new setting took effect. `APPLIED` requires confirmation of the persisted new effective revision; an ActionEvent alone is insufficient. The old basis and every event/change revision remain addressable after reload. Do not mutate a Lab-only override or rewrite accepted evidence. S6-01's Execution/History and applied `changes:1#a` show policy activity with event and configuration provenance, including pending/failed cases; they do not show an effect verdict.
+
+S6-02 owns the separate immutable `VerificationPlanRevision` and later `VerificationOutcome`. A plan pins this Action, the applied-change event and new Foundation revision, a pre-change baseline assessment/evidence basis, target, service/reserve guardrails, required coverage and later-window selection rule before evaluation. Use separate baseline and later Lab runs under their respective fixed effective Foundation revisions; commit their public reports through the ordinary gateway and central acceptance. Evaluate a closed later accepted window against the frozen plan and baseline, with exact analytic/evidence IDs. Missing or conflicting evidence yields `INCONCLUSIVE`; failed guardrail prevents `VERIFIED` even if the target improves; later results append/supersede without rewriting prior outcomes. Plan/evidence/verification child routes preserve exact plan and outcome IDs. `A-S6-verification` remains open for the full comparison rule; this lifecycle decision does not resolve it.
